## Priority backlog

1. Resolve official identity and campus/AISHE code for all 205; manually reconcile aliases before joining records.
2. Collect official website/contact, location, establishment and recognition with exact URLs.
3. Research programme/admissions/eligibility/seat data by year and authority.
4. Research fees and hostel costs by programme/year/category; retain source units and schedules.
5. Research placements with graduating cohort, denominator, median/mean distinction and report year.
6. Research accreditation validity, scholarships, facilities and official notices.
7. Record unavailable/permission-blocked fields as dated dispositions; never fill with guesses.

## Source access decision

AISHE publishes downloadable reports, but its copyright policy says content may not be reproduced without Ministry permission; download permission is not redistribution permission. Do not bulk-import or republish AISHE report contents until written reuse approval or an applicable explicit licence is recorded. JoSAA publishes opening/closing rank downloads, but the checked public pages did not establish redistribution rights; existing access is not treated as reuse permission. NIRF public ranking outputs may be linked and cited, but institution-submitted DCS data is restricted to authenticated institution accounts; do not automate access or redistribute submissions without permission. The project has a local, unreviewed link/fact pilot; provider permission remains use-specific and no bulk source ingestion or production import is authorized by the pilot.

## Status vocabulary

`DEMO` = seed fixture value, not evidence. `MISSING` = no seed field. Future statuses: `SOURCE_BACKED_UNREVIEWED`, `VERIFIED`, `CONFLICT`, `EXPIRED`, `BLOCKED`. Retrieval never changes a claim to verified.

## Fixture pipeline operations

Use a newly verified disposable database and explicit `TEST_DATABASE_URL`/`COLLEGEFIND_TEST_DATABASE_CONFIRMED=true`; the CLI deliberately rejects ordinary runtime DB configuration. Apply additive migrations first. Seed the original demo catalogue if testing the supplied fixture against it.

```
npx tsx scripts/ingest-catalogue.ts --file tests/fixtures/catalogue.json
npx tsx scripts/ingest-catalogue.ts --file tests/fixtures/catalogue.json --apply
```

Default is read-only matching/reporting; `--apply` stages fixture claims atomically with source checksum, import run, rejection issues and audit. Repeated identical content reuses records; changed source content creates a new version and leaves prior evidence intact. No automatic supersession occurs. Ambiguous/unmatched rows appear in the run issues for manual resolution. Canonical College.id/slugs remain unchanged. AISHE and other identifiers are campus-scoped aliases, added only by authenticated admins via `/api/admin/identity`, with source and reason. `NAME` aliases support dated name history. Stable internal College.id remains primary; aliases are not a license to merge campuses.

Visit `/admin/enrichment` as an ADMIN to inspect claims, sources, decisions and runs, stale evidence and fixture flags. The current queue uses database-side filters, stable ordering and bounded pagination; the earlier bounded-screen description is historical. The first owner review packet appears on the dashboard with source evidence and questions. Review reasons are required; decisions are audited. Fixture approval is rejected on the server and fixture evidence is independently excluded by public projection. See the current handoff section at the end of this file; do not use earlier historical counts as current.

Rollback: no destructive down migration is required; roll back application code while retaining additive alias/fixture metadata. Never drop this metadata with staged fixture claims still present. Fixture import failure rolls back claims/source/run/audit together. Do not run on production. Real provider adapter and publication gates remain blocked until explicit source-policy review.

### Source terms and staging archive

The observed pages were publicly readable without authentication. The scope used here is exact URL plus publisher attribution and minimal factual link reference. No automated crawling was performed, no CAPTCHA or access restriction was bypassed, and no full copyrighted document or prose was copied. Successful public viewing does not establish permission for automated collection, storing evidence excerpts/documents or bulk redistribution; those permissions remain unverified provider by provider. Evidence snippets are absent where none were safely retained. Claims remain unreviewed.

The latest import execution was against the labeled disposable PostgreSQL container bound to `127.0.0.1:32776`; no production database was accessed. `data/pilot/local-stage-snapshot.json` now archives139 current claim/source/import-run/audit rows, with zero reviewer decisions and zero fixture claims. After archive verification, that exact local container was removed. The original eight earlier claim IDs remain in their existing result manifest, but their original database audit rows were removed before this pass and cannot be reconstructed. The extra test database slug `preserved` and local test accounts were excluded from the archive. The checked-in205-college seed is unchanged.

All four planned reference milestones (50/100/150/205) are now reached. The next work is not another homepage sweep: it is alternate official-source discovery for the75 blocked/conflicting institutions, campus reconciliation, then field-specific admissions/programme/fee/hostel/scholarship/accreditation/placement/ranking research and authorized human review for all205. No real claim may be promoted from STAGED by retrieval alone.

## Review and publication handoff

Real claims have no human approvals. Review requires an authorized ADMIN in a separately approved local review environment, restoring staged public-source metadata and preserving exported IDs. Inspect exact URLs, domain ownership, university/campus mapping and evidence locator; check scope/expiry; enter a reason and choose approve/reject per claim in `/admin/enrichment`. Approval must be performed by that human; synthetic approvals must never be replayed into real data. Original eight were exported only as manifests/result IDs before their database was removed; their full old audit rows are unavailable here. They remain preserved as original reports, not invented reconstruction. New58 claims have source/run/audit snapshots in `data/pilot/local-stage-snapshot.json` (no users or reviewer decisions); future restoration tooling needs validation before use. This is archived local STAGED evidence, not a running production queue.

Synthetic browser test now exercises unreviewed website exclusion → reviewer API approval → public profile URL/source attribution → rejection → public removal, with DEMO badge preserved and audit counts checked. These actions are explicitly synthetic and do not verify real institutions. Genuine public publication remains pending human review.

## Bounded execution

`npx tsx scripts/ingest-official-references.ts --file <manifest> --offset 0 --limit 25 --apply --report <report>` requires a separately verified disposable test target. Defaults to dry run, max25 rows, checkpointed per-row reports; rejected rows record their index without exposing database/source secrets, later rows continue. Each source/claim/run/audit is atomic; retry identical manifest returns same IDs. Do not use this fixture-safe CLI against production or expand permission scope without review.

Remaining milestones150 and205 are **not executed**. Next research work is the105 NOT_RESEARCHED entries in the batch plan; also revisit the37 source limitations using legitimate alternative pages. No institution has completed all requested field domains. Human review and scoped programme/fee/placement publications remain needed across the entire catalogue; source-reference coverage alone is insufficient.

Earlier batch sources include IIT Bombay’s programme page, IIT Delhi’s 2026–27 UG admissions page, NIT Trichy programme/admissions pages, Central University of Haryana admissions pages, VIT’s campus-labelled programme listing, Graphic Era’s admissions overview, and NIT Mizoram’s programme/admissions pages; exact URLs and page locators are recorded per claim in the JSON and batch manifests.

The pages were manually read from public HTTPS URLs without login or access-control bypass. No provider gave an explicit bulk extraction/document redistribution licence in this research; none is inferred from access. Only concise factual values, source links and locators were kept—no complete PDF, substantial prose or full copyrighted table. A page’s 2026 update marker is not substituted for a publication date. Campus scope was applied where the source was multi-campus (VIT Vellore and MAHE’s Manipal page); those claims still require human identity/campus review. All real claims are UNREVIEWED.

One IIT Bombay 2026–27 fee notice was directly read and captured as a structured research note in batch 001, including separate Indian/foreign applicant components and tuition exemptions. It was **not staged**: the current importer and college-wide fee projection cannot preserve programme/category/component/hostel/waiver distinctions safely. NITK’s fee page exposed 2026–27 tuition and hostel schedule links, but the underlying schedules were not retrieved or parsed, so no NITK fee amount was recorded. IISc’s 2026–27 application deadline had passed at collection and was not restated as current. A current BITS Pilani official admissions URL returned HTTP 502, IIIT Hyderabad’s indexed page returned HTTP 404, NIT Warangal returned a JavaScript-required shell, and Central University of Kerala’s visible admissions links were legacy; no facts from these attempts were staged. No search snippet was treated as source evidence.

All five batches passed importer dry-run, staged transaction and immediate idempotent retry against the independently verified disposable restore database. First application staged 11, 5, 3, 2 and 1 claims; retries reused 11, 5, 3, 2 and 1 respectively without new source/claim records. Final read-back showed 205 colleges, 205 unique slugs, 164 source rows, 169 field claims, 164 import runs, 169 audit events, zero reviewer decisions, zero fixture claims, zero claims without a staging audit, zero orphan sources and zero inverted import timestamps. All 205 restored seed rows remained `UNVERIFIED`; no row was promoted to VERIFIED. These are local clone counts, not production counts.

### Archived snapshot restoration and loss report

The replay manifests contain 147 distinct old claim IDs. The archive has full source/claim/run/audit payloads for 139 IDs and the eight missing IDs are exactly the original pilot entries. On two newly created, empty, independently verified disposable databases, additive migrations and restore completed; repeating restoration kept the same 205 colleges, 147 claims, 147 sources, 147 runs and 147 audit rows. The eight absent records were re-imported through the normal audited importer with **new** claim IDs. Their old audit IDs/history cannot be recovered from available artifacts; no old review decision or approval was fabricated. Archived IDs for 125 claim-bearing catalogue rows were preserved. The source archive does not contain the original College IDs for the other 80 catalogue rows; the test clone generated IDs for those rows, so original-ID recovery for those 80 remains unverified. All 205 seed slugs and the original seed checksum match. Reports: `data/pilot/restore-report-batch-002.json` and `data/pilot/restore-report-repeatability.json`.

A separate restore attempt against a disposable database already populated by the integration fixture correctly stopped on an existing College-ID mismatch; the importer transaction rolled back and wrote no partial restore. Restoration must target a newly provisioned clean clone or one whose 205 identities are already known to match.

### Review/publication gate and backlog

No authorized real reviewer action was available. All 22 substantive claims stay STAGED. A synthetic reviewer action in the disposable integration test and synthetic desktop/mobile Playwright records verified STAGED → approved projection → rejection/public removal, attribution, audit logging, fixture exclusion and demo-value isolation; synthetic decisions are test evidence only and are not counted above as real VERIFIED claims. Until a human reviewer checks each source/campus/scope and records an explicit decision, students must not see these facts as verified.

Next work is substantive official-source research across the remaining 188 institutions without a logged substantive source attempt, starting with the 130 institutions whose official reference pages were readable, plus legitimate alternative discovery for the 75 access-blocked/conflicted entries. Then add a fee-aware typed import/projection for exact schedules; research hostel costs, scholarships, validity-scoped recognition, defined-cohort placement measures, dated rankings and facilities; and arrange authorized reviewer capacity. Keep the 205 original rows and all DEMO/MISSING labels intact until each new field has accepted evidence and review history.

## Source use policy for substantive research

A researcher may manually record a narrowly scoped, independently expressed factual observation from a publicly accessible first-party page when the source identity and evidence are clear, no applicable access or use restriction has been identified, and the record does not reproduce protected expression or a substantial part of a compilation. Examples include a named degree, an individual duration or a dated notice's applicability. Retain the publisher, exact public link, collection time, source date or year when stated, evidence locator and intended-use decision. A copyright footer alone does not establish that an isolated fact is protected. It also does not grant permission to reproduce the page. The [Copyright Act of India](https://ipindia.gov.in/acts/copyright-act-1957) includes tables and compilations within literary works. The [Supreme Court's Eastern Book Company judgment](https://www.wipo.int/wipolex/en/judgments/details/2840) addresses originality and protection for contributions to a compilation.

- Use exact, public source links with attribution. Separate exact programme names and numeric facts from the provider's descriptive prose, layout, graphics, table structure and selection of records. Do not store full notices or reports or recreate substantial tables without a suitable licence or written permission. Research and publication are separate uses. Do not rely on the research fair-dealing exception to justify public redistribution.
- For automated retrieval, check the actual artifact licence and provider terms, robots rules, API constraints and rate limits first. Respect authentication and access denials. Public readability alone provides no bulk retrieval permission.
- Prefer a resource explicitly covered by the [Government Open Data License India](https://data.gov.in/Godl). Record its provider, dataset, version or date, official URL and licence URL. Check exclusions, including personal information and data the provider cannot license. The licence does not attach automatically to every government webpage.
- Hold candidates as research only when scope, authority, terms or compilation risk is material. Request a knowledgeable human or legal review for substantial extraction, conflicting terms, protected content or unclear downstream rights. Use independent first-party sources or a licensed dataset where available. A blocked NIRF page does not justify switching to an unaffiliated mirror or treating a search excerpt as the source.
- Staging an eligible fact records it as unreviewed. Reviewer approval verifies the particular evidence and scope and does not authorize a wider extract or publication of the underlying work.

### Batch 016 facts and dispositions

IIT Kanpur's Dean of Academic Affairs [UG Manual](https://iitk.ac.in/doaa/data/UG_Manual.pdf) was manually opened on 2026-11-18. Its printed pages 5–6 (§§2.1.1–2.1.3) list B.Tech/B.S. programme names and four-year duration, JEE Advanced entry, separate Olympiad routes for specified programmes, and a test/hackathon route for Bachelor in Cybersecurity. Two facts were staged: a concise course list and a bounded admissions summary. Both remain UNREVIEWED with no academic year or publication date asserted. The undated [DoAA programme page](https://iitk.ac.in/doaa/under-graduate-programme) omits Intelligent Systems from the B.Tech list; this discrepancy is explicitly recorded in the claim locator and batch manifest and must be resolved by a human reviewer before approval. The PDF is not copied or redistributed. No exact publication date or explicit reuse licence was located in this check; the record contains the factual summary, link and locator only.

Two follow-ups did not produce stageable claims:

- **IIT Roorkee:** its official [UG Ordinance](https://iitr.ac.in/Academics/static/Notifications/UG%20Notifications/72.5_UG_Ordinance_BoG.pdf) contains a detailed programme/duration table, but the earlier official Academic Information page differs. This remains held for source-recency and conflict review; no list was staged.
- **NIT Warangal:** its official [Biotechnology programme page](https://beta.nitw.ac.in/biotech/academic-programmes/) states a four-year B.Tech, the JEE Main/JoSAA/CSAB route, and a PCM condition. Its [website policy](https://nitw.ac.in/cdis/itpolicy) permits direct links without prior permission but says reproduction of site material requires permission by email and source acknowledgement. These facts remain research-only until written permission or a reviewed legal determination resolves whether the narrow factual extraction may be stored and published.

The batch manifest is `data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-016.json`; reports are `data/pilot/substantive-batch-016-{dry-run,import,idempotency}-report.json`. Dry-run validated two candidates. Import staged 2 claims, created 1 source and 1 import run; identical retry staged 0, reused both claims, and created no records. No review decision was added.

### Existing substantive claim audit and reviewer queue

The archive-level audit covers all **132** substantive facts (the 147 official-link references are not counted as substantive facts): 132/132 have a source record with HTTPS URL, publisher, retrieval time and evidence locator; all are non-fixture; none has a reviewer decision. This is metadata/provenance validation, **not** a human accuracy review of all source contents or campuses. Existing evidence was matched to the original College row through stable IDs/slugs, and the importer enforces manifest institution-slug/source consistency. The older 130 facts have not all been manually re-opened against the institution and campus; no claim is called verified on this basis.

Prioritized reviewer actions in the authenticated dashboard (`/admin/enrichment?status=STALE`, `/admin/enrichment?status=CONFLICT`, then `PENDING` with search by college slug):

1. **Recollect or reject three expired admissions claims; do not approve them as current:** IISER Pune `cmwlti52o000cq2tv8t32n2hv` (valid through 2026-04-30), IISER Pune `cmwlti531000gq2tvv330tpph` (2026-04-20), and IISER Kolkata `cmwlti53g000lq2tvjxlh9l14` (2026-06-07). Their source links and historical year are retained.
2. **Reconcile source discrepancies before approval:** both new IIT Kanpur claims carry the UG Manual vs undated DoAA-page caveat; IIT Roorkee's programme conflict remains research-only and is not among staged claims.
3. **Review cycle-sensitive claims next:** structured fees and admissions require checking academic year, programme, category, fee component, counselling route and source date. Do not flatten hostel/tuition or category scopes.
4. **Resolve dating gaps:** 39 of the 132 claims have neither a claim academic year nor a source publication date. For stable facts, reviewers may record why year does not apply; for admissions, fees, rankings or other cycle-bound facts, hold until dated evidence is available.
5. Review remaining programme, scholarship, contact and ranking facts individually for canonical campus, exact source publisher/domain, evidence locator, currency, conflicts and source-use conditions. Record explicit approval/rejection reason; one field decision must not verify adjacent fields.

The snapshot has zero same-field/scope/year conflicting staged value groups by the database queue's definition. That does not rule out disagreement between un-staged official pages, as the IITK and IIT Roorkee dispositions demonstrate. All 132 remain staged/unreviewed. The restore database has no reviewer accounts or decisions, so genuine review requires an authorized CollegeFind reviewer account and actual human decisions; synthetic E2E/integration approvals do not count.

## Human-review handoff — 287 staged claims (2026-11-18)

The admin queue is authorized and paginated. Its default STAGED view orders substantive claims ahead of website/admissions links; use **ALL** to inspect every state, and filter by college, campus/city/state, field, academic year and evidence freshness. This report identifies **132 substantive claims to open and assess**, not to approve automatically. Six expired cycle claims and two IIT Kanpur discrepancy claims are held; the remaining 147 records are link references and require identity/domain review. Genuine approvals: 0. No authorized reviewer account is present in the archived snapshot.

### Review sequence and decision questions

1. Inspect non-held substantive admissions and fee claims first; verify cycle/year, programme/campus, exam/category, fee unit and validity. Then scholarships and rankings; then programmes/courses; then scoped contacts. The table carries a claim-specific check prompt and exact official publisher URL/evidence locator.
2. Do not approve the six expired IISER/CUTN records or two IIT Kanpur records. Resolve each expiry with a fresh source or explicitly reject/retain as historical; reconcile the IIT Kanpur DoAA Manual vs undated UG programme-list discrepancy first. Preserve historical claim and audit records.
3. Inspect the 147 website/admissions references after substantive facts. Confirm domain ownership, campus applicability and redirects. A link reference confirms no programme, fee, eligibility or current admissions fact. Public access does not itself establish permission for document storage, automated extraction or bulk redistribution.
4. For any decision, open the exact source link, match the evidence locator, verify field scope and recency, consider conflicts/supersession, then record an individual reviewer reason. Only a human reviewer can approve.

MBBS duration 4.5 academic years plus 12-month internship; 2026 MBBS admission/eligibility details; annual tuition ₹3,000; one-time hostel deposits/advances ₹10,000 each for men and women. No deadline/open status is asserted. Hostel amounts are one-time deposit/advance, not annual room cost. MBBS source/campus and current-cycle applicability require human review. |

### Source-use review for this batch

Both institutions' pages were public and directly opened; that establishes accessibility, not permission for bulk extraction or republishing. No PDF was copied into the repository and no substantial source prose/table was stored. CMC's consulted prospectus contained no matching reproduction notice; its separate [Intellectual Property Policy](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/intellectual-property-policy/) is a patenting/process page and does not establish document reuse. Terms for CollegeFind's concise fact summaries remain unresolved pending human review.

IIMB's [published copyright policy](https://www.iimb.ac.in/copyright-policy) states that IIMB site content is protected and that partial or full reproduction requires permission, with attribution if material is referred to in another publication. The staged records contain concise factual summaries, exact source URLs and locators rather than source prose or documents. The policy is not interpreted as automatically prohibiting individual facts, nor as granting a right to bulk extraction. Hold the three claims for a human source-use/legal determination before public approval. No permission request or approval is claimed.

- Batch 019 processed 25 already-researched NIRF 2025 Engineering candidates after typed rank support became available: 25 staged, then 25 reused on retry. This was not a new college research pass.

- The explicit `TEST_DATABASE_URL` confirmation was already set for the CollegeFind disposable target. Independent read-back before batch 022 identified database `collegefind_phase3_restore`, role `cf_phase3_test`, server endpoint inside its isolated container, 205 colleges, 334 claims, zero users and zero reviewer decisions. No production or DevSync database was accessed.
- Apply: `npx tsx scripts/ingest-official-facts.ts --file data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-022.json --apply --report data/pilot/substantive-batch-022-import-report.json` — **PASS**, eight staged, two sources and two import runs created; all claims UNREVIEWED.
- Immediate identical retry with `--apply` — **PASS**, zero staged/eight reused, zero new sources/runs, `databaseChanged=false`.
- Snapshot: `npx tsx scripts/export-staging-snapshot.ts --name staged-evidence-after-batch-022` — **PASS**. Exact read-back export: 205 colleges, 247 sources, 342 claims, 271 runs, 342 audits; zero users, decisions or fixtures. Slug/identity checksum is unchanged; all staged evidence retains source and audit references.
- The first sandbox-only dry run failed because `tsx` could not create its temporary IPC pipe (`EPERM`). The same read-only command passed after permission escalation. No code changed, so lint/typecheck/build/browser suites were not rerun; last application verification remains the recorded prior passing result. ### Dashboard access and secure owner setup

The dashboard is implemented and enforces ADMIN authorization. The archive deliberately has no accounts. `/api/auth/register` creates only a USER; there is no in-app role escalation or reviewer-account provisioning. Therefore **the project owner cannot yet sign in to the real review queue from this checkout until an owner provisions local reviewer access**. Unauthenticated users are redirected to login and a normal USER receives Access denied.

Safe local procedure: (1) create a new isolated local review database from the latest snapshot; do not reuse the archive/production/DevSync DB; (2) configure local-only `DATABASE_URL`, `NEXTAUTH_URL=http://localhost:3000`, and a locally generated `NEXTAUTH_SECRET` in an untracked `.env.local`; (3) start the app and register the owner’s own account through `/register` (it is created as USER); (4) using the local database owner only, independently verify `current_database()`, `current_user`, and the loopback host/port, then promote only that owner-created account to ADMIN using the repository’s `User.role` field; (5) log out/in and visit `/admin/enrichment`; confirm the account name in the session and test a harmless filter before reviewing. Never run the role update against an unverified database. Keep credentials out of shell history, reports and Git. No account or role change was made in this task.

### Publication workflow evidence

The existing desktop/mobile Playwright reviewer suite was run against the separate disposable browser-test DB: `npx playwright test tests/e2e/admin.spec.ts --reporter=list` — **2 passed**. It verifies unauthenticated and USER denial, admin filters/pagination/conflict view, fixture-approval denial, synthetic STAGED→approval→public typed-programme projection, rejection, and audit-oriented decision flow. `npx playwright test tests/e2e/comparison.spec.ts --reporter=list` — **15 passed, 1 intentional skip**; this covers comparison restore/current server values, null/demo exclusion, saved-college flows and mobile navigation. A previous unchanged smoke run passed 14 tests, including the predictor’s no-official-cutoff empty state. Synthetic reviewer approval is test-only and does not count as human verification; no explicit mixed-null CollegeFit browser case was run in this increment.

### Reviewer dashboard and the first human review session

The authenticated dashboard is at `/admin/enrichment`. It supports database-side filtering/pagination, stable ordering, search, college/campus/field/year/freshness filters, conflict context, source-opening links, reviewer history, and explicit approve/reject decisions with a reason. Claim cards expose institution and campus context, field/value, academic year/category, publisher, URL, source locator, freshness and review state. Decision writes require ADMIN authorization and are audited. The same 25-claim prioritized packet already documented above remains the first owner review batch; its exact claim IDs, values, source links, locators, campus notes and recommended reviewer questions are in `humanReviewManifest.firstHumanReviewBatch.claims` in the JSON ledger. The 16 held claims are separate from that packet.

**Real owner login is not yet available in this checkout.** Registration creates a USER and the restored archive intentionally contains no users. Do not use the archive database for real decisions. Safe local-only owner setup:

1. Create a fresh isolated local review database and restore the latest snapshot using the documented disposable-database guard. Verify database name, role and loopback connection before setting the explicit test confirmation; do not reuse production, DevSync, or a valuable development DB.
2. Configure a local-only, untracked `.env.local` with `DATABASE_URL` for that verified review DB and `NEXTAUTH_URL=http://localhost:3000`. Generate/store a fresh local `NEXTAUTH_SECRET` without printing it into logs or committing it.
3. Start the app and register the project owner’s own account at `/register`. Registration creates role `USER`.
4. Recheck `current_database()`, `current_user`, and the local host/port. Using the local database owner and only after verifying that target, promote only the owner-created account. A parameterized form of the update is `UPDATE "User" SET role='ADMIN' WHERE lower(email)=lower($1) AND role='USER' RETURNING id,email,role;` with the owner’s own email bound as `$1`.
5. Log out and back in, visit `/admin/enrichment`, confirm the owner’s identity in the session, then test a harmless filter. Review each claim’s institution/campus, source, scope/year, freshness/conflicts and source-use basis before explicitly approving or rejecting it with a reason.

No owner account was created or promoted in this work. No genuine reviewer decision exists.

### First owner-review packet (20–30 claims)

The existing 25-claim ordered packet remains unchanged and is ready for inspection, not preapproval. It prioritizes current programme/admissions and year/category-scoped fee claims from CMI, IIM Calcutta, SPJIMR, Nirma, DAU, IIT Roorkee, MNNIT and CMC Vellore. Use the packet table above and JSON `firstHumanReviewBatch.claims` for the exact claim values and sources. Open each official link in a separate tab and check the cited section/page before deciding. Some entries are intentionally narrow: CMC MBBS duration includes internship; IIT Roorkee ₹100,000 is tuition only; MNNIT placement rows are placed-count denominators, not salary packages. Never infer that one approved fact verifies other fields for the same college.

All 16 new claims across batches 025–027 are STAGED/UNREVIEWED, not genuine approvals. Each manifest records exact source URL, publisher, retrieval time, applicable year, evidence location and access/reuse notes: [`batch 025`](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-025.json), [`batch 026`](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-026.json), [`batch 027`](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-027.json). Batch 027 covers 20 targeted dispositions; 15 yielded no stageable current fact. For example, no fees were staged from old Bennett 2025 tables, no current admissions advice was staged from closed Andhra/Calcutta 2026 cycles, and no facts were attached to ambiguous multi-campus Amity/GITAM or mismatched Central University pages. NIT Agartala, NIT Srinagar, Amrita, Bennett and Calcutta claim IDs/import outcomes are in `data/pilot/substantive-batch-027-import-report.json`.

Source-use remains field/provider specific. Direct reading of a public official page was not treated as an automated scraping or bulk-republication licence. Only short attributed facts and locators were stored; no source documents or full tables were copied. No signed AIIMS download URL was retained. Blocked or ambiguous sources remain explicit in the batch manifests.

### Publication verification and exact checks

- `npx playwright test tests/e2e/admin.spec.ts --reporter=list` on the verified separate browser DB after adding the fee-publication assertion — **2 passed** (desktop Chromium and mobile). It exercises auth denial, admin review, conflicts/search/pagination, fixture protection, staged-to-approved synthetic programme publication, rejection/audit, and now a synthetic fee that is absent before approval and visible afterward with year, category, amount and source link. Synthetic actions are test-only.
- Previously passing unchanged-code evidence remains applicable: integration suite **63/63** on a separate verified disposable DB; comparison suite **15 passed, 1 intentional skip**; smoke suite **14 passed**. They were not repeated because application/schema code did not change. The new assertion changes only the browser test file.
- Batch 026 dry run: **2 facts PASS**; disposable DB read-only identity/count preflight matched batch 025 snapshot; apply: **2 staged, 2 sources/runs created**; retry: **0 staged, 2 reused, no database change**.
- Batch 027 dry run: **11 facts PASS**; disposable DB read-only identity/count preflight matched batch 026 snapshot; apply: **11 staged, 5 sources/runs created**; retry: **0 staged, 11 reused, no database change**.
- Batch 027 snapshot export: **PASS**, 205 identities, 269 sources, 377 claims, 293 runs, 377 audits, zero users/decisions/fixtures. SHA and referential integrity are checked by the existing exporter. The stable identity checksum is unchanged.
- `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `git diff --check`, and JSON/snapshot reconciliation results are recorded in the latest `UPGRADE_STATUS.md` section.

No production or DevSync database, production setting, credential, push, merge or deployment was accessed or modified.

## Historical owner-review clone record — superseded

The 2026-11-18 clone `collegefind_phase3_snapshot_owner_review_20261118_test` was hosted in the disposable CollegeFind test container on port 35439. It held 205 colleges, 269 sources, 377 staged claims, 293 import runs and 377 staging audits, with zero users and decisions at that time. This was a restore/test clone, **not a persistent owner-review database**. The old startup and ADMIN-promotion commands that used this target have been withdrawn; do not use that clone to make genuine review decisions.

For the current owner handoff, use [`OWNER_EVIDENCE_REVIEW.md`](OWNER_EVIDENCE_REVIEW.md). It keeps test safeguards separate, documents a guarded persistent local restore path, owner account creation and one-account ADMIN promotion, review checklist, encrypted full-database backup and safe restore. No owner database has been selected or provisioned in this task.

### First genuine review packet and local access

The 25-claim packet remains in [`first-human-review-batch.json`](../data/pilot/first-human-review-batch.json) and is rendered in `/admin/enrichment`. The clone identity and counts described in the following historical section were checked on 2026-11-18, but that database was a disposable `_test` restore clone, not durable owner storage. **Do not register, promote an account or write genuine review decisions to that clone.** The former command block that targeted it has been withdrawn. The current owner setup, target contract and local review steps are in [`OWNER_EVIDENCE_REVIEW.md`](OWNER_EVIDENCE_REVIEW.md); owner provisioning is still pending. The 16 held claims remain excluded.

### Phase 3 decision and Phase 4 boundary

**Phase 3 engineering: COMPLETE WITH EXPLICIT DATA/OWNER BLOCKERS.** The identity, staging, archive/restore, reviewer safeguards, public projection and fixture-isolation engineering gates have recorded local test evidence. This is not a statement that source facts are approved or the catalogue is fully enriched. Research coverage remains partial and source reuse is unresolved provider-by-provider.

**Phase 4 locally: CONDITIONAL GO.** Safe to begin local profile/cutoff engineering only if it consumes verified projections, renders unreviewed/expired/missing facts with their correct states, preserves demo isolation, and stays on disposable/local data. No factual publication/production release is authorized; no real claim is verified. Phase 4 gates depending on genuine reviewed records remain blocked until the owner reviews evidence.

Outstanding release blockers: owner must provide non-secret evidence of historical credential revocation/rotation, protected seed-route configuration, production Redis rate limiting and outage behavior, and database backup retention plus successful restore readiness. No production check was performed.

### Phase 4 cutoff coverage baseline — 2026-11-19

The 205-college research ledger and manifests contain **0 staged official JoSAA/MCC/state cutoff claims and 0 genuinely verified cutoff claims**. The 25 NIRF 2025 candidates are institutional rankings, not admission cutoffs. No production or owner-review database was queried, and no real cutoff information was imported during this Phase 4 code increment. Synthetic cutoff rows used by disposable integration/browser tests do not count toward institution or field coverage. See the machine-readable `phase4CutoffCoverage` object in [`DATA_COVERAGE.json`](./DATA_COVERAGE.json). The cutoff explorer therefore honestly starts in its empty state until official records are collected, reviewed and approved.

The typed cutoff model/query now represent counselling system, exam, year, round, quota, category, PwD, seat pool, metric, rank type, opening/closing value, preparatory rank, programme, college, source URL/publisher/publication/retrieval dates, reviewer-decision time and optional `CutoffRecord.evidenceLocator`. The additive migration stores the source row/page location (the JoSAA CSV adapter records a CSV line number); it does not copy copyrighted source text. Existing rows migrate to `UNSPECIFIED` system/rank type rather than guessed values. The `/cutoffs` explorer includes a scoped opening/closing rank trend chart with a complete accessible table alternative. These additions improve traceability and presentation but do not change the official data count: source material still requires reviewer inspection.

### Cutoff explorer scale verification — 2026-11-19

The disposable integration fixture exercised 61 and 2,001 approved **synthetic** rows over 12 colleges/programmes, four synthetic sources, multiple years, exams, counselling systems, categories, quotas, seat pools, rounds and rank types. Stable ordering, the final one-row page, out-of-range page clamping, filter counts, source attribution, rank values, legacy `UNSPECIFIED`, and API validation were checked against independent expected results. These records are test-only and do not alter the official cutoff counts above.

The run exposed an expensive repeated latest-review lookup. The additive `EvidenceDecision(cutoffId, createdAt DESC, id DESC)` index was added and applied to the disposable database; EXPLAIN ANALYZE used this index for latest-decision retrieval. With the final source-locator projection, 10 warmups and 100 local samples per query, nonselective page 1 latency was p50/p95/p99 **15.125/16.088/24.942 ms**; page 21 **19.227/20.194/23.257 ms**; selective page **3.392/3.910/4.158 ms**; broad count **7.219/12.584/13.166 ms**; selective count **1.879/2.818/3.061 ms**. In the broad 2,001-row plan PostgreSQL sequentially scanned the small synthetic cutoff table (2,001 rows), while the selective plan used `CutoffRecord_exam_category_seatPool_year_idx`; latest decisions used `EvidenceDecision_cutoffId_createdAt_id_idx`. These are warm local synthetic measurements, not production performance claims.

JoSAA currently exposes an official opening/closing rank listing and a previous-years archive with year, round, institution, programme and category dimensions. Its current official page defines OPEN as CRL, reserved categories as their category rank, PwD ranks within the category, and the `P` suffix as a preparatory rank list ([current official OR/CR page](https://josaa.admissions.nic.in/Applicant/SeatAllotmentResult/CurrentORCR.aspx), [JoSAA OR-CR listing](https://josaa.nic.in/or-cr/)). The JoSAA reproduction policy says reproduction requires proper permission from NIC and stakeholders and separately protected third-party material needs its copyright holder's permission ([JoSAA copyright policy](https://josaa.admissions.nic.in/Applicant/FooterPages/copyrightPolicy.aspx)). Public viewing therefore supports linking and source research, but does not establish permission for automated extraction, storing complete source tables, or bulk republication. No JoSAA records were imported; source-use and permitted storage/republication remain unresolved pending written permission or a clearly licensed official dataset. No restriction was bypassed.

### Phase 4 trend and accessibility verification — 2026-11-19

The trend is generated only from the same PostgreSQL projection as the cutoff table: latest decision must be APPROVED, the source must be HTTPS with publisher attribution, and the college must not be DEMO. It requires one exact college and one unambiguous programme plus counselling system, rank type, exam, category, quota, seat pool, PwD setting and `RANK` metric. Scores/percentiles are never mixed into rank trends. The complete year/round series is fetched without a chart-specific result cap. Missing combinations are explicit table rows and break the SVG path; missing opening ranks remain “Not published”; there is no extrapolation. Every observation retains source URL, publisher, academic year, publication/retrieval/review dates and optional evidence location.

Latest relevant verification: `npm test` **PASS, 114/114 unit tests**; the cutoff-explorer and JoSAA PostgreSQL integration files **PASS, 18/18** on the independently verified disposable CollegeFind database; serial Playwright smoke/admin suites **PASS, 24/24** across desktop Chromium and Pixel 7. The browser cases cover reviewer auth/approval/rejection/audit, fixture isolation, filter sharing and Back/Forward/refresh, invalid and empty filters, keyboard filtering/focus/live result announcements, profile-to-cutoff navigation, source/table/chart accessibility semantics, and narrow viewport overflow. `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `npx prisma generate` and `npx next build --webpack` all passed. Integration performance used the 2,001-row synthetic case above. These automated results do not constitute a full manual screen-reader audit or real official-data review.

The build and browser server used `/tmp/collegefind-phase4-migration-disposable.env`, whose database identity was independently checked before destructive tests; no production/DevSync resource was used. An initial unapproved sandbox integration attempt failed before SQL with `connect EPERM`; the same suite passed under host permission. The build also passed under host permission; with the verified database environment sitemap generation completed without the fallback warning. Local Playwright logs show the intentional in-memory rate-limit fallback because test Redis credentials are absent; this is not production Redis evidence.

Cutoff row/page evidence locations are now available for new importer rows through nullable `CutoffRecord.evidenceLocator`; legacy rows remain null and unchanged. Migration `20261119030000_cutoff_source_locator` is additive. The disposable migration DB has eight applied migrations and its existing 2,001 synthetic test rows were preserved across this migration. No migration rollback was executed; rollback is a forward-compatible additive-column reversal only after confirming no importer/client depends on the field. No real cutoff candidate was staged or approved: official staged/genuine VERIFIED counts remain **0/0**.

### Catalogue follow-up order

1. Start with the existing first owner review packet. Group by institution and source, opening each source once and checking each claim separately. Where a new cohort is published, retain the older source as historical.
2. Take the 82 colleges in the existing substantive backlog in groups of roughly 20. Apply the next source action from each ledger row. Start with an official programme or admissions page, then pursue a fee schedule, accreditor decision or cohort report with comparable definitions. For medical colleges require campus-specific counselling and seat category. For engineering use institution or counselling authority material and keep course offerings separate from cutoffs. For management use the specific programme, campus and intake policy. For universities and private institutions use the department and campus, plus the relevant statutory or accrediting authority where available.
3. Review the 123 colleges with staged substantive material in source groups, prioritizing current programme, scoped fee, eligibility and recognition claims. Skip held entries until their recorded restriction, expiry or conflict is resolved. Source-only references need a domain and campus check independently of academic facts.
4. After the owner records genuine decisions, compare approved values to public profile sections, search, comparisons and CollegeFit where the field is supported. Check dates, units and scope. Validate eligible cutoff publication separately when reviewed cutoff records exist.

### What counts as an informative profile

Classification measures what a student can read on the page. A basic profile shows catalogue identity and may link to external sources. A partially informative profile has at least one visible, reviewed substantive domain. A meaningfully informative profile has reviewed programme or department detail, a scoped admissions route or eligibility rule, costs or aid with the relevant period, and enough support for the institution's main study decisions. Institutional identity and scope must match. Recognition, contacts, facilities and outcome data improve completeness when applicable. Links, staged records and import validation do not upgrade the class.

| Institution | Evidence needed for its main student decisions |
| --- | --- |
| Engineering | Campus and branch, degree and duration, entrance exam and counselling category, fee schedule, labs or housing. When available, placements should give the graduating class and metric, and cutoffs should give pool and round. |
| General university | Faculty or department and campus, exact degree and mode, intake requirements, fee or aid schedule, relevant recognition and student services. |
| Medical | College and teaching location, degree and internship where applicable, current regulator or university affiliation, official seat and counselling scope, and category-specific fees or bond conditions where they apply. Engineering-style placements are optional. |
| Law | Exact integrated or graduate route, duration, entrance or merit route and category, current approval or affiliation where applicable, and programme fee schedule. |
| Management | Exact degree and campus, residential, hybrid or other delivery mode, admissions cycle and test, cohort fee and aid, and outcomes with cohort and year. |
| Arts and science | Programme, subject stream and shift, aided or self-supporting scope, selection or prerequisites, and relevant fees, aid and facilities. An entrance cutoff is optional where no such process exists. |

[Batch 031](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-031.json) checked 12 directory-only institutions. Ten new candidates across seven colleges passed the database-free dry run, and six source issues were held. The source guide gained 13 destinations on eight profiles. Seven of those profiles gained first-party pages and WBNUJS Kolkata gained the official Consortium of NLUs participation reference. A timeout or oversized brochure did not become a programme claim. Other holds record an exact fetch failure or blocked prospectus.

All 205 profiles are currently classified as basic. The number with direct institutional or authority resources rose from 131 to 139, with 66 directory-only profiles remaining. There are still 377 staged records and 230 substantive staged claims across 123 institutions. No new persistent evidence, genuine decision or official cutoff was added. Exact values, source locations, scope, freshness and rights notes for new research are in the batch. The completeness ledger records the individual differences.

Batch 028's seven candidates retain their source-use and freshness blocks. Batch 029's nine candidates still require explicit authorization of the exact manifest and owner destination. Batch 030's four candidates still need scope, freshness and rights checks, including the historical Andhra University cycle. Batch 031 excludes already-staged Ashoka admission, NISER entrance route and IIM Nagpur MBA programme facts. It remains disabled for persistent staging pending source-specific checks and owner authorization.

## Publication of programme periods and facilities, Batch 033

Local profile coverage grew from 20 to 62 of 205 institutions. The release adds 45 cards, bringing the total to 67. Students can see 64 programme records and three facilities records. IIT Bombay, NIT Tiruchirappalli and NIT Calicut have both categories. The other 143 profiles have basic identity or navigation. The per-college ledger lists each published fact, campus, publisher, source URL, source date and evidence location.

The additions draw on 23 archived claims, one Batch 029 claim and 21 independently sourced or reformatted Batch 033 candidates. Batch 033 records individual facts from first-party pages. It carries a source-use assessment for each source. Three facilities cards cover campus accommodation or library presence. They provide no housing guarantee, prices or capacity claims. Decisions about financial figures, admissions and eligibility, accreditation, placements and cutoffs remain in the stronger review workflow.

Historical listings include the closed 2026–27 COEP Bachelor of Planning cycle and the 2025–26 St. Xavier’s College handbook. Other cards retain named periods, including SPJIMR’s 2027–29 cohort and Central University of Kerala’s 2026–27 programme listing. Expired release checks and explicit fact validity limits still prevent publication.

Batch 028 retains its seven holds. The Jamia prospectus exceeded the browser fetch limit and its reproduction question remains open. MICA timed out, Loyola returned 403, and the original LNMIIT admissions URL timed out. The LNMIIT department page independently substantiates the five-year integrated CSE programme. The GNLU and AFMC source restrictions remain unresolved. Andhra University’s historical notice and other unselected candidates remain outside the release, as do the IIT Kanpur conflict and Ashoka fee records. [Publication manifest](../data/pilot/source-reported-release.json) · [Batch 033](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-033.json) · [College-level coverage](PROFILE_COMPLETENESS.json)

## Content-depth increment — batches 034–036

The previous checkpoint showed 67 source-reported facts across 62 profiles (64 programmes, three facilities), three multi-category profiles and 143 profiles without substantive source-reported detail. This increment adds **59** source-reported facts across **41 additional profiles**, reaching **126 facts across 103 profiles**. Of the 59, 51 are newly researched candidate facts from batches 034–036; eight are previously archived or importer-validated facts that can now render in the admissions/contact sections. They were not imported to persistent owner-review storage. All remain labeled “Source-reported; not independently verified.”

| Student-facing category | Before | After | Delta |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Programmes/degrees | 64 | 99 | +35 |
| Facilities | 3 | 17 | +14 |
| Admissions routes (no eligibility thresholds) | 0 | 7 | +7 |
| Institutional contacts | 0 | 3 | +3 |
| Fees, scholarships, recognition, placements, rankings, cutoffs | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Profiles with any source-reported fact | 62 | 103 | +41 |
| Profiles with at least two distinct categories | 3 | 21 | +18 |
| Identity-only/basic profiles | 143 | 102 | -41 |

The new candidates are 35 programme facts, 14 facilities facts and two dated admissions-route facts. Contact and additional admissions cards account for the eight archived/earlier-manifest facts newly renderable. These counts describe the current checked source-reported register, not human-approved coverage. The full 205-institution status remains in [`PROFILE_COMPLETENESS.json`](PROFILE_COMPLETENESS.json), including per-college category counts and missing categories. A second-category signal does not make a profile complete. The profile content criteria differ by institution type: engineering profiles need branch/degree, admission path, cost and comparable cohort information; medical profiles need programme, regulator, admission and clinical/training scope; law profiles need degree, admissions route, regulator scope and legal-learning context; management profiles need programme/cohort, admission process, total cost and cohort outcomes; general universities need campus-specific degrees, applicable admission routes, fees/aid and relevant academic/facility context.

The newly researched programme candidates include IIT Delhi CSE curriculum, MNIT Architecture, IIT-BHU Mining Engineering, NIT Rourkela Civil Engineering, IIM Shillong PGP, NLIU Bhopal B.A. LL.B., St John’s MBBS and several other official department/programme pages. Facilities include named campus libraries at NLU Delhi, IIT Delhi, NIT Meghalaya, NIT Hamirpur and others. The admission examples are CMI’s explicitly future 2027–28 examination/application route and NIT Goa’s explicitly historical 2026 JEE Main/JoSAA/CSAB route. [Batch 034](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-034.json) · [Batch 035](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-035.json) · [Batch 036](../data/pilot/substantive-research-batch-036.json) · [Source-reported register](../data/pilot/source-reported-release.json)

Only short normalized factual labels are shown with direct official attribution; source pages and documents are not copied into CollegeFind. The absence of a licence is not treated as blanket permission for bulk extraction or reproduction. Pages are rechecked on a short expiry window and can be withdrawn by the release manifest; archive evidence holds, explicit negative decisions, conflicts, mismatched campus scope and expired source checks suppress cards. Admission-route cards omit eligibility and application deadlines and cannot influence verified-only search, comparisons, CollegeFit or probability guidance. Fees, scholarship terms, regulator validity, placements, rankings and cutoffs remain unavailable unless supported through stronger evidence review. No external page access restrictions were bypassed.

Two recognition leads remain held: Tezpur’s NBA register was inaccessible (HTTP 403) and available institutional corroboration was not readable (newly recorded in this increment); St John’s NMC course register requires current-validity reconciliation (existing hold retained). Other blocked primary-source attempts included 403, 502, timeout and non-readable PDF responses for specific programme/admission/facilities sources; exact URLs and outcomes remain in the batch artifacts where a factual candidate was captured, or the research disposition on the affected college row. No values were inferred from snippets or peer institutions.

The existing evidence archive remains 377 STAGED claims (230 substantive plus 147 reference claims), with zero genuine human approvals and zero verified cutoff records. New factual database staging in this increment: **0**. New facts shown through the source-reported register: **59**. This is a distinct status and is not counted as VERIFIED.

Browser verification for this increment passed in Chromium desktop and Pixel 7 mobile: each project checked HTTP 200 and the expected institution heading for all 205 canonical college routes, then rendered and checked the source-reported cards across all 103 profiles/126 facts. It checked exact source URLs, campus, evidence locators, period labels, historical cycle marking, absence from verified API/search inputs, the unverified fee empty state, and horizontal overflow. Screenshots of NALSAR’s historical CLAT route, IIT Delhi Central Library, and PES University’s admissions contact are available at `/tmp/cf-profile-depth-20261121/` (`nalsar-admissions-{desktop,mobile}.png`, `iit-delhi-facilities-{desktop,mobile}.png`, `pes-contact-{desktop,mobile}.png`). The directory is local test evidence and is not part of the persistent review database.

Batch 039 adds 16 facts on Lovely Professional University (two programme names and four civil-school laboratory facilities), JIPMER Puducherry (MBBS and its historical 2025–26 NEET/MCC route), University of Mumbai (four Department of English programme listings), and SRM Institute of Science and Technology (four School of Law programmes with source-listed durations). The LPU brochure has no stated academic year, so its programme and facility entries are labeled historical and do not imply current intake or facility availability; its Phagwara scope is cross-checked against the brochure address. The JIPMER prospectus expressly distinguishes Puducherry and Karaikal as separate admission entities; the new route is historical and scoped accordingly. The University of Mumbai department page gives no academic year or physical teaching campus, both disclosed in the scope. The SRM School of Law programme page gives no academic year or campus. Jadavpur University’s official page returned HTTP 502 on direct opening; its search-result text was not used as evidence.

Every displayed item remains labeled “Source-reported; not independently verified” and links to the exact official page with its evidence location and retrieval/check date. No whole prospectus, brochure, table, eligibility threshold, fee amount, seat count, placement outcome, ranking or cutoff was republished. Public access was not treated as a blanket licence. No persistent review database was modified: archive staging remains **377 claims** (230 substantive), genuine human approvals remain **0**, and verified official cutoff records remain **0**. None of the 38 items is counted as VERIFIED or used by search scoring, comparisons, CollegeFit or admission-probability guidance.

The same run confirmed fee figures remain unavailable, the held IIT Kanpur claim remains absent, and source-reported facts do not appear in the verified public API. ## Structured comparison projection — 2026-11-23

The comparison page now consumes the same per-college source-reported projection as profile pages and presents programmes/degrees, admission routes, facilities and contacts in an attributed source-reported table. It preserves college/campus, academic year, historical state, retrieval date, evidence location and direct source URL. It does not convert those records into verified comparison metrics. The existing reviewer-approved structured-fact section and unknown fee/placement metrics remain separate. Current inventory remains 503 source-reported facts across 205 profiles: programmes 278 records / 174 colleges; admissions 39 / 37; facilities 86 / 72; contacts 100 / 86. Genuinely human-reviewed fees, eligibility, placements, scholarships and official cutoffs remain 0. No new claims were collected or written to the owner-review database in this code-only slice.

Production-mode browser comparison was checked on desktop and Pixel 7 mobile with IIT Bombay and IIMA. Both source-reported route values, seven supporting links across the two colleges, labels, applicable year/scope and unavailable values were present; no page-level horizontal overflow occurred. Screenshots are `/tmp/cf-compare-structured-desktop.png` and `/tmp/cf-compare-structured-mobile.png`. A new E2E regression case covers the separation and attribution contract; it was not executed because this task had no independently verified disposable PostgreSQL test target.
